PREFACE
(in original edition)

This work, which is now offered to the public, is believed to be the first attempt at a complete Dictionary of  the Tamil language in alphabetical order. It contains about 58,500 words being nearly four times as many as are found in the whole rJufuhâ.
When the work was commenced the Compilers contemplated preparing little more than a Vocabulary for the use of Schools.  This will account for the size of the Appendix in the vowels, and in f.
It is not supposed to be free from defects, but it is believed that the work will be highly valuable not only to those who read the Christian Scriptures in the Tamil language but to those who study the higher branches of Tamil literature.
AMERICAN MISSION PRESS,
JAFFNA, 1842



muṉṉurai (mūla nūl) : tamiḻākkam
tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ colvaḷattai akaravaricaiyil muḻumai yākat tarum mutal muyaṟciyāka uruvākkappaṭṭa ivvakarāti tamiḻulakiṉ muṉṉar camarppikkappaṭukiṟatu. ivvakarātiyil 58,500 coṟkaḷ uḷḷaṉa; atāvatu caturakarāti muḻuvatilum aṭaṅkiya coṟkaḷaiviṭa nāṉku maṭaṅku coṟkaḷ.
paḷḷi māṇava māṇaviyar payaṉpaṭuttum coṟṟokup pākat tāṉ ippaṇi mutaṟkaṇ toṭaṅkap peṟṟatu. uyir mutaṟ coṟkaḷiṉ iṇaippum kakarac coṟkaḷiṉ iṇaippum caṟṟu nīḷamāka uḷḷataṟku atutāṉ kāṇaram.
innūlil piḻaikaḷ cila irukkalām. eṉiṉum kiṟittava maṟai nūlkaḷait tamiḻil payilpavarkaḷukku maṭṭumaṉṟi tamiḻ ilakkiyattiṉ palvēṟu nuṭpamāṉa tuṟaikaḷil payilvōrukkum ivvakarāti perumpayaṉ taruvatāka irukkum eṉṟu nampap paṭukiṟatu.
1842.	viṭaiyūḻiyar,
amerikkaṉ accakam, yāḻppāṇam,
 


īḻam tanta tamiḻk koṭai
īḻam tanta tamiḻkkoṭai peritu. īḻattup pūtantēvaṉār caṅka kālattavar. akkālattiṟkup piṉṉark kālam kālamākat tamiḻ vaḷartta muṉṉōṭiyarum pulamaic cāṉṟōrum ēṟattāḻa irunūṟṟuvar uḷar. avaruḷ akarāti, kalaik kaḷañciyam eṉpavaṟṟai muṉṉiṉṟu ceytu vaḻikāṭṭiya perumakkaḷ maṭṭum ivaṇ cuṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.
“īḻam tanta iṉiya tamiḻk koṭai” eṉṉum peyarāl, īḻattut tamiḻc cāṉṟōr perumakkaḷ taṉi nūlākkam peṟukiṉṟaṉar.
ne. cēṉāti rāya mutaliyār (1750 - 1840)
māṉippāy amerikkamicaṉ tokutta akarātit tokaiyāḷaruḷ ivarum oruvar.
caimaṉ kācic ceṭṭi (1807 - 1861)
tamiḻp pulavarkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟai akarāti muṟaiyil varicaip paṭutti āṅkila nūl ceytavar ivar.
ivar ceyta innūlē pulavar varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷil muṟpaṭṭatu eṉpar. kūyaaṭai ñaṭaravayacauh eṉpatu ataṉpeyar. vinōtaraca mañcaripōl kaṭṭuk kataiyallāta varalāṟu atu.

a. catācivap piḷḷai (1820 - 1896)
caimaṉ kācic ceṭṭi āṅkilattil ceyta tamiḻp pulavar varalāṟṟu nūlai virivu ceytu ‘pāvalar carittira tīpakam’ eṉṉum peyaril tamiḻ moḻi peyarppāka veḷiyiṭṭavar ivar (1886)
cantira cēkara paṇṭitar (19ām nū.ā)
caturakarātiyai aṭuttuvanta akarāti yāḻppāṇa akarāti ākum. ataṉai māṉippāy akarāti eṉṟum colvar. ataṉai ākkutaṟkup peritum uḻaittavar ippaṇṭitar.
pīṟṟar percival
īḻattil vāḻnta ivar, tamiḻp paḻamoḻikaḷait tokuttu āṅkila moḻipeyarppuṭaṉ veḷiyiṭṭār. āṅkila - tamiḻ akarāti oṉṟum veḷiyiṭṭār.
ku. katiraivēṟ piḷḷai (1829 - 1904)
coṟkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam, ilakkiya āṭci mutaliyavaṟṟaik kāṭṭip pērakarāti ceytavar. maturait tamiḻc caṅkap paṇikku avvakarātiyāl perumai cērttavar.
nā. katiraivēṟ piḷḷai (1844 - 1907)
ivarum pērakarāti oṉṟu ceytavar.
ā. muttut tampip piḷḷai (1852 - 1917)
ivar iyaṟṟiya apitāṉa kōcam eṉpatu tamiḻil tōṉṟiya kalaik kaḷañciya nūlkaḷukku muṉṉōṭiyāṉatu (1902) āṅkilam - tamiḻ akarāti oṉṟum ivar ceytār.
cuvāmi ñāṉap pirakācar (1875 - 1947)
tamiḻaip piṟamoḻikaḷoṭum oppiṭṭu ‘ulakat talaimoḻi tamiḻē’ eṉpatai niṟuviyavar. coṟpiṟappiyal akaramutali amaittu veḷiyiṭṭavar. ‘tamiḻ amaippuṟṟa varalāṟu’ varaintavar.
na.ci. kantaiyā piḷḷai (1893 - 1967)
centamiḻ akarāti, tamiḻp pulavar akarāti, tamiḻ ilakkiya akarāti, kālak kuṟippu akarāti, tirukkuṟaḷ collaṭaivu akarāti eṉap pala akarātikaḷaic ceyta ariya periya uḻaippāḷar.

irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār

 


patippurai
akarāti eṉpatu, kaṟpārkkut tuṇainiṉṟu uriya poruḷ terintu koḷḷa vaḻivakuppatu; kalvi kaṟpikkum āciriyariṉ nilaiyil uḷḷatu akarāti. āciriyariṭam nēril māṇavar kalviyiṉ payaṉaip peṟuvatupōla akarātiyiṉ tuṇaiyāl aṟivu viḷakkam peṟalām. akarāti eṉpatai ‘moḻiñāyiṟu’ tēvanēyap pāvāṇar avarkaḷ  ‘akaramutali’ eṉṟu peyariṭṭu mutaṉmutalil tamiḻ ulakil puḻakkattukku viṭṭa perumaik kuriyavar.
mutal tamiḻ akarāti - caturakarāti
18ām nūṟṟāṇṭil ittāli maṇṇiliruntu kiṟittava camayam parappat tamiḻmaṇṇukku vantavar peski pātiriyār eṉṉum vīramāmuṉivar. nikaṇṭukaḷilum, pā vaṭivilum amaintirunta coṟporuḷ viḷakkaṅkaḷai mutaṉmutalāka urai naṭaiyil māṟṟi ‘akarātiyāka’ 1732ām āṇṭu paṭaittaḷittār. ip paṭaippiṟkuc caturakarāti eṉap peyariṭṭār. ip paṭaippu 18ām nūṟṟāṇṭil iyaṟṟappeṟṟa pōtilum muḻumaiyāka 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭil 1824il tāṉ accil  veḷivantatu.  tamiḻ akarāti varalāṟṟil tiruppumuṉaikku aṭikōliyavar vīramāmuṉivar.  
tamiḻīḻa aṟiñar na.ci. kantaiyāviṉ aṉaittu nūlkaḷaiyum tēṭi eṭuttu  orē nērattil amiḻtam patippakam  veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu. ivar nūlkaḷil oṉṟāṉa kālak kuṟippu akarātiyaip paṭikka nērntapōtu, 1842il cantira cēkarap paṇṭitar maṟṟum caravaṇamuttup piḷḷai avarkaḷāl  tokukkappaṭṭa māṉippāy akarāti eṉṉum yāḻppāṇa akarātiyaip paṟṟiya kuṟippu kiṭaittatu.  

tamiḻ akarātikaḷukku aṭittaḷam yāḻppāṇa akarāti
yāḻppāṇattaic cērnta māṉippāy eṉṉum ūril acciṭappeṟṟatāl ūrppeyaraic cuṭṭi ippeyar iṭap peṟṟatu pōlum. caturakarātiyiṉ mutal akarātiyākiya peyarakarātiyiṉ virivē yāḻppāṇa akarātiyākum.
19ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ maiyap pakutiyil 1842il ivvakarāti veḷivantatu. veḷivanta kālattil tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ nilaiyum, im moḻiyiṉpāl makkaḷiṉ pārvaiyum eppaṭi iruntaṉa eṉpatai aṟiya utavuvatu yāḻppāṇa akarāti.. vaṭamoḻi vallāṇmai mēlōṅki irunta kālattil veḷivanta akarāti yātalāl mūlattil evvita māṟṟamum ceyyap paṭāmal marapu karuti uḷḷavāṟē veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷōm. ‘aṉupanta akarāti’ eṉṟa piṉṉiṇaippu intap patippil akaravaricaippaṭi avvav viṭattil cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
tāymoḻiyām tamiḻmoḻi uyarcciyil paṟṟuṭaiya aṉparkaḷ aṉaivarukkum payaṉpaṭumpaṭi ivvakarātiyai  veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷōm.
aṇmaik kālattil tamiḻp patippulakil paḻantamiḻ akarātikaḷ paṭaaccup patippāka paravalāka veḷi vantuḷḷa cūḻalil, iṉṟaiya aṟiviyaliṉ tuṇai  koṇṭu accut tuṟaiyiṉ vaḷarcci mēlōṅki irukkum nilaiyil kaṇiṉi vaḻi puttuyir peṟṟu mikac ciṟanta vaṭivamaippuṭaṉ, innūliṉait tamiḻ kūṟum ulakiṟku putiya varavāyk koṭut tuḷḷōm. tamiḻ akarātikaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyai virumpuvārkkup paḻaiya akarātikaḷ vaḻikāṭṭum oḷiviḷakkāy amaiyum. nam pāṭṭaṉ tanta aruntamiḻc celvattait tēṭi eṭuttut tamiḻarkkuk karuvūlamāyt tantuḷḷōm.
tamiḻ iḷantalaimuṟaikkum, āyvāḷarkaḷukkum, kallūri, palkalaikkaḻaka nūlakaṅkaḷukkum payaṉpaṭum eṉum nōkkil 2006il veḷivanta ivvaruntamiḻ pērakarātiyai mīḷ patippāka ivvāṇṭu veḷiyiṭukiṟōm.
  tamiḻar tam nākarikap paṇpāṭṭuk kūṟukaḷait tamiḻarkkuk koṇṭu cērkkum perum paṇiyai tolainōkkup pārvai yuṭaṉ em patippuc cuvaṭukaḷai patittu varukiṟōm. ulakeṅkum parantu vāḻum em tamiḻp pēriṉam akara mutali tuṟaikku tiṟavu kōlāka uḷḷa ivvaruntamiḻ akarātiyai vāṅkip payaṉaṭaiyum eṉṟu nampukiṟōm. ip paḻantamiḻp peṭṭakam putuppolivōṭu varuvataṟku tōḷōṭu tōḷ niṉṟu uḻaitta aṉaivarukkum em naṉṟi.

patippāḷar

pākam – 1

aḵkulli - ciṟṟuṇṭi, piṭṭu.

akaṅkarittal - āṅkāraṅkoḷ ḷutal.
akaṅkarippu - maṉaveṟuppu.
akaṅkaḷittal - maṉamakiḻtal.

akaṅkaḷittal - maṉamatarttal.
akaṅkārattirayam - mukkiyavakaṅ kāram, mukkiyā mukkiyavakaṅ kāram, amukkiyavakaṅ kāram eṉamūṉṟu.

eḻuttiṉōcai - akkaram viḷakki niṟkuntoṉi.

eḻuttukkāriyastaṉ - eḻuttuk kāraṉ.

ē - aṭē, icainiṟai, īṟṟacai, eṇṇiṉkuṟippu, (um) nilaṉē nīrē, etirmaṟai, (um) poyyē, ōreḻuttu, tēṟṟakkuṟippu, (um) nāṉēvantatu, pāṇam, piriviṉ kuṟippu, (um) ivaṟṟuḷoṉṟē naṉṟu, viṉākkuṟippu (um) taralē vantatu.

ai - aracaṉ, aḻaku, ācāṉ, iraṇṭa ṉurupu, irumal, ecamāṉaṉ, ōriṭaiccol, ōreḻuttu, kaṭavuḷ, kuru, kōḻai, cāriyai, (um) ēṟṟiṉōṭēṟṟai, cuvāmi, nuṇmai, pitā, yāṉaiyaip pākaṉ taṭṭumōcai, civaṉ, muṉ ṉilaiyorumai viṉaivikuti, (um) vantaṉai, toḻiṟ peyar vikuti, (um) naṭai, paṇpuppeyar vikuti, (um) tollai, viṉai mutaṟ poruḷ vikuti, (um) paṟavai, ceyappaṭuporuḷ vikuti, (um) toṭai, karuvip poruḷ vikuti, (um) pārvai.

orupavam - orupiṟappu.
orupāvorupaḵtu - ōr pirapantam. aḵtu akaval veṇpā kalit tuṟai yeṉumivaṟ ṟoṉṟālan tātit toṭaiyāyp pattuc ceyyuṭpāṭuvatu.
orupuṭai - orupakkam.
orupuṭaiyoppumai - ēkatēcavuvamam.

oruporuṭpaṉmoḻi - oruporu ḷaik kuṟittu varum palacol.
oruporuḷ - ēkārttam, kaṭavuḷ.
oruporuḷ kuṟitta palapeyarttiri col - centamiḻnilattu moḻi kaḷāyc ceyyuṭkēyuriya vāyk kaṟṟōrkku māttiram poruḷ viḷakkiṉavāy oṉṟaṉ peyarai yē viḷakkum palapeyarttiri col, (um) kiḷḷai, tattai.
oruporuḷkuṟitta palaviṭaittiri col - centamiḻ nilattu moḻi kaḷāyk kaṟṟōrkku māttiram poruḷaiviḷakki oru poru ṭarum palaviṭait tiricol, (um) eṉēṉ.
oruporuḷ kuṟitta palaviṉait tiricol - centamiḻnilattu moḻi kaḷāyk kaṟṟōrkku māttiram poruḷ viḷakkittiri collāy niṟkum palaviṉait tiricol (um) carittāṉ, naṭantāṉ.

kāntaḷ - kārttikaippū, cavukkāram.

kār - accam, aḻaku, āvaṇi puraṭ ṭātiyiṉ paruvam, iruḷ, karuṅ kuraṅku, karumai, kāreṉ ṉēval, kārnel, nīr, mēkam, veḷḷāṭu, eli, ēriṟpūṭṭiya virumpāṇi.

ku
ku - iṉmaikkum etirmaṟaikkum varumōrupacaṟkam, ōreḻuttu, kuṟṟam, ciṟumai, taṭai, toṉi, nāṉkāmurupu, nintai, pāvam, pūmi, irutiṇaippālorumait taṉmai viṉaivikuti, (um) uṇku, cāriyai, (um) varukuti, paṇpup peyarvikuti, (um) naṉku, piṟa viṉaivikuti, (um) pōkku.

kēḷvuttōṇi - kūlikkup porun tiṉatōṇi.


kai
kai - iṭam, uṭaṉē, oppaṉai, oḻukkam, ōreḻuttu, ōrvikuti (um) kaṭuṅkai, karam, kai koṇ ṭaḷavu, kaiyeṉṉēval, ciṟumai, taṅkai, toṉi, pakkam, paṅku, paṭaivakuppu, yāṉait tutikkai.
kaikaṭattal - kaiyaiviṭṭuppōtal.
kaikaṭaṉ - īṭuṟutiyillākkaṭaṉ.
kaikaṭṭutal - mariyātaikkākakkai kaṭṭiniṟṟal.
kaikaṇṭacāram - navacāram.
kaikaṇṭatu -cittiyuḷḷatu, tēṟiṉṟu.
kaikam - vakkirānti pāṣāṇam.
kaikalattal - oṉṟāykūṭutal, yuttaṅ kalattal.
kaikaḻuvutal - kuṟṟattukku vilaku mōrkuṟippu, tīrttuttaḷḷal.
kaikāṭṭu - kaiyiṉālapi nayaṅ kāṭṭal, caikaikāṭṭal.
kaikāṭṭutal - apinayaṅkāṭṭal, kaiyāṟkuṟippukkāṭṭal.
kaikāṇutal - attāṭcippaṭutal, nampikkaiyāyaṟital, niṟaivēṟu tal.
kaikāval - kaiyutavi.
kaikīḻ - kaiyamaiccal, kaiyuṟuti.
kaikuvittal - kumpiṭal, kommai koṭṭal, vaṇaṅkal.
kaikuḷirtal - tiruttiyātal.
kaikuṟaṇṭutal - ulōpittaṉam, kaimuṭaṅkal.
kaikūṭutal - cittiyātal.
kaikūppal - kumpiṭal, kompai koṭṭal, vaṇaṅkal.
kaikūppu - aintumuḻaṅ koṇṭa vaḷavai, kaikuvippu.
kaikūpputal - kumpiṭal.
kaikūmputal - kaikuvital.
kaikēci - irāvaṇaṉṟāy.
kaikēyaṉ - ōraracaṉ.
kaikēyi - parataṉṟāy.
kaikoṭuttal - utaviceytal, etirkoṇṭu poyk kaipiṭit taḻaittal, kaikūṭutal, vākkup paṇṇal.
kaikoṭṭal - kommai koṭṭal.
kaikoṭṭikkuvittal - kuṭantam.
kaikōttal - kūṭikkoḷḷutal, kaiyōṭukaipiṭittal.
kaikōttāṭal - kuravaiyāṭal.
kaikōvai - poṟkollar toḻiliṉoṉṟu.
kaikōḷaṉ, kaikkōḷaṉ - ney vārilōr cātiyāṉ.
kaikōḷ - oḻukkattiṉṟaṉmai.
kaikkaṭaṉāṟṟal - uṟutiyiṉṟi nampikkaikkuk koṭuttal, atu vēḷāṇmai māntariyalpu ḷoṉṟu.
kaikkaṭṭi - ōrāparaṇam, kaik kalacam.
kaikkaṭṭu - ōrāparaṇam.
kaikkaṇakku - ōruṟuti.
kaikkaṇicam - kaimatiyam.
kaikkatti - ōrāyutam.
kaikkaraṇai - oru cilanti.
kaikkavacam - kaiccaṭṭai.
kaikkāṇikkai - veḷippāṭu.
kaikkāyccal - kaikkānti.
kaikkāraṉ - utaviyuḷḷavaṉ, paṇak kāraṉ.
kaikkiriyai - kaiyiṉāṟceyyuṅ kiriyai.
kaikkiḷai - ēḻicaiyiṉoṉṟu, atu aṇṇattāṟ piṟakkumicai, oru talaikkāmam, oru pirapantam. aḵtu orutalaikkāmattiṉai aintuviruttattāṟ kūṟuvatu veṇpāmuppattiraṇṭāṟ kūṟalumām, yāḻiṉōr narampu.
kaikkiḷaimaṇam - ācuramirākkatam paicācamūṉṟum.
kaikkīṟu - kaiyeḻuttiṉ kuṟippu.
kaikkuṭṭai - ciṟukuṭai.
kaikkuṭṭai - kaiyiṟpiṭikkum puṭaivaittuṇṭu.
kaikkuttu - kaipottikkuttal.
kaikkunīrkkoḷḷutal - ciṟunīr viṭutal.
kaikkuḻantai - iṭukkuppiḷḷai.
kaikkuḻalūtal - kaiyilēkuḻalūtal.
kaikkuḻi - kamukkaṭṭu.
kaikkuḻittāmarai - oru viyāti.
kaikkuḷ - uṭaṉē, caṭuti.
kaikkuṟi - kaivaraipārkkuñ cāttiram.
kaikkuṟṟam - kaimōcam.
kaikkuṉṟu - yāṉai.
kaikkūccam - kaikūcutal.
kaikkūli - paritāṉam.
kaikkeḷiyatu - ilēcāṉatu, kīḻa maiccalāṉatu.
kaikkai - kacappu.
kaikkoṭṭai - kaiyaṇai.
kaikkoḷḷutal - aṅkīkarittal.
kaikkōṭāli - ciṟukōṭāli.
kaikkōraṇi - kaiccittiram.
kaikkōl - ūṉṟukōl.

kaicaruvutal - kaikalattal, kaic caracam.

kaicikam - iccai, ōrirākam, mayirk kaṉam, vikāram.
kaiciyam - kēcam.

kaicōram - pāliyam.
kaiccaṅkam - nākaravaṇṭu.

kaiṭavai - turkkai.
kaitaṭṭal - kaiyoṭukai taṭṭal, kaiviṭutal, tapputal.
kaitapputal - nīṅkal.
kaitarutal - iṭaṅkoṭuttal, utavu tal, kaikūṭutal.
kaitavam - tuṉpam, poy, vañcaṉai, pantayam.
kaitavaṉ - pāṇṭiyaṉ.
kaitaḷartal - kainekiḻtal, cōrtal, toytal.
kaitāṅki - kaipiṭi, nāṟkāliyi ṉōruṟuppu.
kaitūkkal - iraṭcittal, viṭutalai yākkal.
kaitūvāmai - oḻiyāvoḻukkam.
kaitai - tāḻai, vayal.
kaitaiccurikaiyaṉ - maṉmataṉ.
kaitoṭal - uṇṇal, kaliyāṇam.
kaittalam - kai.
kaittal - alaṅkarittal, alaittal, kacattal, kōpittal, veṟuttal.
kaittaḷai - kaivilaṅku.
kaittākku - kaikkukkaṉam, kaik kuttu, kaivēkam.
kaittāy - vaḷarkkuntāy.
kaittāḷam - kaimaṇi.
kaittiṭṭam - kaimatiyam.
kaittīvatti - kaiyiṟpiṭikkun tīvaṭṭi.
kaittīṉ - kaiyil vaittukkoṭuk kuntīṉ.
kaittu - poṉ, veṟuppu.
kaittuppākki - kaittuvakku.
kaitturuvumaṇai - pirayāṇat turuvumaṇai.
kaittuvakku - ciṟutuvakku.
kaittūkku - kaippaṟṟam, kaiyāṟ ṟūkkattakkapāram.
kaittēṅkāy - pōraṭikkuntēṅkāy.
kaittoṇṭu - kuṟṟēval.
kaittoḻil - kaivēlai.
kaittoḻumpu - kaittoṇṭu.
kainnaṭukkam - oru viyāti, kaiyiṟuṅ kāmai.
kainnaṭṭam - mutalkūṭanaṭṭappaṭutal.
kainnalappāl - kaṉṟiṉṟikkaivalla pattāṟ kaṟakkumpāl.

kaipiṭi - irammiyam, uṟuti, kai piṭikka vaittiruppatu, kaip poruḷ, kaiviṭāmai, nampik kai, nāṭiyōṭṭa maṟital.
kaipiṭittal - kaliyāṇam muṭittal, nāṭiyaṟital.
kaipiṭipattiram - uṟṟaciṉēkam, kai viṭāmaṟ pattiramāyiruttal.
kaipiṭiyāṉavaṉ - uṟṟaciṉēkitaṉ.
kaipiḻaipāṭu - kaimōcam.
kaipukuntavaḻakku - kaiyil vantu viṭṭateṉṟu ṭaiyavaṉukku millai yeṉpatu.
kaipuṉai, kaipuṉaital - alaṅ karittal, pūttoṭuttal.
kaipōṭutal - kaiyiṭutal, vākkup paṇṇal.
kaippaṭṭai - kaippalakai, ciṟu paṭṭai.
kaippaṇam - kaimmēṟpaṇam, kaiyilirukkum paṇam.
kaippaṇi - kaṟciṟpar karuviyi ṉoṉṟu, kaittoṇṭu.
kaippataṭṭam - kaḷḷam.
kaippantam - pantaviḷakku.
kaippayil - kaiccayikkiṉai.
kaippaḻakkam - kaippariṭcai.
kaippaṟṟam - kaippiṭi, kaivāṟu.
kaippaṟṟal - kaliyāṇam, kaivacatti loṭukkal, vāṅkutal.

kaippāṭu - kaivacam.
kaippāti - iraṇṭāyp piḷappatiṟ ciṟu pāti.

kaiyaṟuti - kaiyuṟutiyilē viṟkap paṭṭa vaṟuti.
kaiyaṟunilai - oru pirapantam. aḵtu kaṇavaṉōṭu maṉaivi kaḻin tuḷiyavar kaṭpaṭṭa aḻivup poruḷellāvaṟṟaiyum piṟark kaṟivuṟuttiyiṟantu paṭātoḻinta āyattārum paricil peṟum viṟavi yarun taṉippaṭaruḻanta ceyalaṟu nilaiyaik kūṟuvatu.
kaiyaṟai - oḻukkamiṉmai, ceya liṉmai.
